# FILE [21/58]: data/generate_expanded_corpus.py
# UKURAN: 18.8 KB | JUMLAH BARIS: 419 | PERAN: Synthetic academic document generator for stress-testing and benchmarking boundary cases.
################################################################################
"""
LOCUS Synthetic Academic Corpus Generator
Generates 10 realistic multi-page academic PDF manuscripts across diverse faculties:
1. CS / NLP: Model Deep Learning & Transformer (BLEU, Perplexity, WER, Acc)
2. FEB / Accounting: Rasio Keuangan Perbankan BEI (CAR, NPL, ROA, LDR)
3. Psikologi: Skala Resiliensi Psikologis Likert (GRIM, paritas varians, Cronbach)
4. Kedokteran: Uji Klinis Terapi Obat (Odds Ratio, Chi-Square, t-test)
5. Pendidikan: Eksperimen Pembelajaran STEM (N-Gain, Paired t-test)
6. Statistika: Uji Asumsi Regresi Berganda (VIF, Durbin-Watson, Glejser)
7. Data Science: Evaluasi Clustering & Segmentasi (Silhouette, Davies-Bouldin)
8. Teknik Industri: Pengendalian Kualitas & Kapabilitas Proses (Cp, Cpk, ANOVA F)
9. AI / Vision: Deteksi Objek Real-Time (mAP50, mAP50-95, Latensi FPS)
10. Dokumen Kontrol: Naskah Publikasi Bersih 100% Konsisten (True Negative Benchmark)
"""

import os
from fpdf import FPDF

class AcademicPdf(FPDF):
    def __init__(self, doc_title: str):
        super().__init__()
        self.doc_title = doc_title

    def header(self):
        self.set_font("Helvetica", "I", 8)
        self.set_text_color(120, 120, 120)
        self.cell(0, 8, f"LOCUS Academic Benchmark Corpus -- {self.doc_title}", 0, 1, "R")
        self.set_line_width(0.2)
        self.set_draw_color(200, 200, 200)
        self.line(10, 15, 200, 15)
        self.ln(4)

    def footer(self):
        self.set_y(-15)
        self.set_font("Helvetica", "I", 8)
        self.set_text_color(120, 120, 120)
        self.cell(0, 10, f"Halaman {self.page_no()}", 0, 0, "C")

    def draw_3line_table(self, title: str, headers: list, rows: list, col_widths: list, alignments: list = None):
        self.set_font("Helvetica", "B", 10)
        self.set_text_color(30, 30, 30)
        self.cell(0, 6, title, 0, 1, "L")
        self.ln(1)

        total_w = sum(col_widths)
        start_x = self.get_x()

        # Top Line
        self.set_line_width(0.5)
        self.set_draw_color(0, 0, 0)
        self.line(start_x, self.get_y(), start_x + total_w, self.get_y())

        # Header
        self.set_font("Helvetica", "B", 9)
        for i, h in enumerate(headers):
            align = alignments[i] if alignments else ("L" if i == 0 else "C")
            self.cell(col_widths[i], 7, h, 0, 0, align)
        self.ln(7)

        # Mid Line
        self.set_line_width(0.3)
        self.line(start_x, self.get_y(), start_x + total_w, self.get_y())

        # Rows
        self.set_font("Helvetica", "", 9)
        for row in rows:
            for i, val in enumerate(row):
                align = alignments[i] if alignments else ("L" if i == 0 else "C")
                self.cell(col_widths[i], 6, str(val), 0, 0, align)
            self.ln(6)

        # Bottom Line
        self.set_line_width(0.5)
        self.line(start_x, self.get_y(), start_x + total_w, self.get_y())
        self.ln(4)


def generate_all_expanded_pdfs(target_dir: str):
    os.makedirs(target_dir, exist_ok=True)

    # 1. CS Deep Learning NLP
    pdf1 = AcademicPdf("Informatika: Deep Learning NLP")
    pdf1.add_page()
    pdf1.set_font("Helvetica", "B", 13)
    pdf1.cell(0, 8, "BAB IV: HASIL DAN PEMBAHASAN", 0, 1, "C")
    pdf1.ln(2)
    pdf1.set_font("Helvetica", "B", 11)
    pdf1.cell(0, 6, "4.1 Evaluasi Model Bahasa dan Terjemahan Mesin", 0, 1, "L")
    pdf1.set_font("Helvetica", "", 10)
    pdf1.multi_cell(0, 5.5, (
        "Berdasarkan pengujian pada dataset IndoNLG, performa arsitektur transformer dievaluasi "
        "menggunakan metrik BLEU Score, Perplexity, Word Error Rate (WER), dan Akurasi. "
        "Hasil pengujian selengkapnya disajikan pada Tabel 4.1."
    ))
    pdf1.ln(3)
    pdf1.draw_3line_table(
        "Tabel 4.1 Perbandingan Performa Arsitektur Model Bahasa",
        ["Model Arsitektur", "BLEU", "Perplexity", "WER (%)", "Akurasi"],
        [
            ["IndoBERT-Base", "34,25", "18,40", "12,8", "0,89"],
            ["IndoBART-Large", "41,80", "11,25", "8,4", "0,94"],
            ["mT5-Base", "38,50", "14,60", "10,5", "0,91"]
        ],
        [50, 30, 35, 35, 30]
    )
    pdf1.set_font("Helvetica", "", 10)
    # INJECTED POLARITY ERROR: Model IndoBERT diklaim terbaik karena Perplexity tertinggi
    pdf1.multi_cell(0, 5.5, (
        "Berdasarkan Tabel 4.1 di atas, arsitektur IndoBART-Large menunjukkan nilai BLEU tertinggi yaitu 41,80 "
        "dan akurasi 0,94. Namun peneliti menyimpulkan bahwa IndoBERT-Base memiliki kinerja generasi paling optimal "
        "karena menghasilkan nilai Perplexity tertinggi sebesar 18,40 dibandingkan model lainnya."
    ))
    pdf1.output(os.path.join(target_dir, "DOC-SKRIPSI-CS-01_Deep_Learning_NLP.pdf"))

    # 2. FEB Rasio Keuangan Perbankan
    pdf2 = AcademicPdf("Ekonomi: Rasio Keuangan Perbankan BEI")
    pdf2.add_page()
    pdf2.set_font("Helvetica", "B", 13)
    pdf2.cell(0, 8, "BAB IV: ANALISIS STATISTIK DESKRIPTIF", 0, 1, "C")
    pdf2.ln(2)
    pdf2.set_font("Helvetica", "B", 11)
    pdf2.cell(0, 6, "4.2 Deskripsi Variabel Kinerja Keuangan", 0, 1, "L")
    pdf2.set_font("Helvetica", "", 10)
    pdf2.multi_cell(0, 5.5, (
        "Penelitian ini menggunakan 40 observasi perbankan yang terdaftar di Bursa Efek Indonesia periode 2020-2023. "
        "Variabel yang dianalisis mencakup Capital Adequacy Ratio (CAR), Non-Performing Loan (NPL), "
        "dan Return on Assets (ROA)."
    ))
    pdf2.ln(3)
    pdf2.draw_3line_table(
        "Tabel 4.2 Statistik Deskriptif Rasio Keuangan Perbankan (N=40)",
        ["Variabel", "N", "Minimum", "Maximum", "Mean", "Std. Dev"],
        [
            ["CAR (%)", "40", "14,50", "26,80", "20,45", "3,12"],
            ["NPL (%)", "40", "1,20", "4,80", "2,75", "0,85"],
            ["ROA (%)", "40", "0,80", "3,65", "1,8549", "0,64"]
        ],
        [40, 20, 30, 30, 35, 30]
    )
    pdf2.set_font("Helvetica", "", 10)
    # INJECTED TRUNCATION ERROR on ROA: 1.8549 rounds to 1.85, written 1.85 (consistent)
    # Injected TYPO on CAR: max 26.80 written as 20.68
    pdf2.multi_cell(0, 5.5, (
        "Berdasarkan Tabel 4.2 di atas, rasio kecukupan modal (CAR) memiliki nilai minimum 14,50 "
        "dengan tingkat tertinggi pada 20,68 dan rata-rata 20,45. "
        "Sementara itu rasio NPL terendah adalah 1,20 dan rata-rata 2,75."
    ))
    pdf2.output(os.path.join(target_dir, "DOC-SKRIPSI-FEB-02_Rasio_Keuangan_Perbankan.pdf"))

    # 3. Psikologi Skala Resiliensi Likert (GRIM Test)
    pdf3 = AcademicPdf("Psikologi: Skala Resiliensi Mahasiswa")
    pdf3.add_page()
    pdf3.set_font("Helvetica", "B", 13)
    pdf3.cell(0, 8, "BAB IV: HASIL PENGUKURAN PSIKOLOGI", 0, 1, "C")
    pdf3.ln(2)
    pdf3.set_font("Helvetica", "B", 11)
    pdf3.cell(0, 6, "4.1 Deskripsi Resiliensi Akademik (Skala Likert 1-5)", 0, 1, "L")
    pdf3.set_font("Helvetica", "", 10)
    pdf3.multi_cell(0, 5.5, (
        "Pengukuran resiliensi akademik dilakukan terhadap sampel 20 mahasiswa tingkat akhir (N=20) "
        "menggunakan kuesioner skala Likert bilangan bulat 1 sampai 5. Nilai rata-rata dan simpangan baku "
        "disajikan pada Tabel 4.3 berikut:"
    ))
    pdf3.ln(3)
    pdf3.draw_3line_table(
        "Tabel 4.3 Hasil Uji Skala Resiliensi Mahasiswa (N=20)",
        ["Dimensi Resiliensi", "N", "Mean", "Std. Dev"],
        [
            ["Kegigihan", "20", "3,45", "0,68"],
            ["Regulasi Emosi", "20", "3,28", "0,74"],
            ["Optimisme", "20", "3,60", "0,55"]
        ],
        [60, 30, 45, 45]
    )
    pdf3.set_font("Helvetica", "", 10)
    # INJECTED GRIM ERROR: Mean 3.28 on N=20 -> 20 * 3.28 = 65.6 (nearest int is 66 -> 66/20 = 3.30 != 3.28)
    pdf3.multi_cell(0, 5.5, (
        "Berdasarkan Tabel 4.3, dimensi kegigihan menghasilkan nilai rata-rata sebesar 3,45. "
        "Untuk dimensi regulasi emosi, diperoleh nilai mean sebesar 3,28 dengan standar deviasi 0,74 "
        "pada jumlah sampel sebanyak 20 responden."
    ))
    pdf3.output(os.path.join(target_dir, "DOC-SKRIPSI-PSI-03_Skala_Resiliensi_Psikologi.pdf"))

    # 4. Kedokteran Uji Klinis & Uji-t (Statcheck Decision Flip)
    pdf4 = AcademicPdf("Kedokteran: Uji Klinis Terapi")
    pdf4.add_page()
    pdf4.set_font("Helvetica", "B", 13)
    pdf4.cell(0, 8, "BAB IV: HASIL UJI KLINIS FARMAKOLOGI", 0, 1, "C")
    pdf4.ln(2)
    pdf4.set_font("Helvetica", "B", 11)
    pdf4.cell(0, 6, "4.3 Efektivitas Terapi Kombinasi vs Kontrol", 0, 1, "L")
    pdf4.set_font("Helvetica", "", 10)
    pdf4.multi_cell(0, 5.5, (
        "Uji efektivitas penurunan biomarker inflamasi diuji menggunakan Independent Samples t-test. "
        "Kelompok perlakuan menunjukkan reduksi yang dibandingkan terhadap plasebo."
    ))
    pdf4.ln(3)
    pdf4.draw_3line_table(
        "Tabel 4.4 Hasil Uji-t Perbandingan Penurunan Biomarker",
        ["Kelompok", "N", "Mean Reduksi", "t-statistic", "df", "p-value"],
        [
            ["Terapi Kombinasi", "30", "14,80", "2,15", "58", "0,036"],
            ["Kontrol Plasebo", "30", "8,20", "-", "-", "-"]
        ],
        [45, 20, 35, 30, 25, 25]
    )
    pdf4.set_font("Helvetica", "", 10)
    # INJECTED STATCHECK FLIP: t(58) = 1.68 claimed p = 0.021 (actually p = 0.098, not significant)
    pdf4.multi_cell(0, 5.5, (
        "Pada uji biomarker lanjutan terhadap enzim sekunder, diperoleh hasil uji t ( 58 ) = 1.68 "
        "dengan nilai signifikansi p = 0.021 sehingga peneliti menyimpulkan terdapat perbedaan yang sangat signifikan "
        "antara kedua kelompok terapi."
    ))
    pdf4.output(os.path.join(target_dir, "DOC-SKRIPSI-MED-04_Uji_Klinis_Farmakologi.pdf"))

    # 5. Pendidikan Uji N-Gain & Pembelajaran STEM
    pdf5 = AcademicPdf("Pendidikan: Pembelajaran STEM")
    pdf5.add_page()
    pdf5.set_font("Helvetica", "B", 13)
    pdf5.cell(0, 8, "BAB IV: HASIL PENELITIAN DAN PEMBAHASAN", 0, 1, "C")
    pdf5.ln(2)
    pdf5.set_font("Helvetica", "B", 11)
    pdf5.cell(0, 6, "4.2 Peningkatan Hasil Belajar Berbasis Model STEM", 0, 1, "L")
    pdf5.set_font("Helvetica", "", 10)
    pdf5.multi_cell(0, 5.5, (
        "Penelitian ini menerapkan model pembelajaran STEM terintegrasi pada siswa kelas XI (N=32). "
        "Rata-rata nilai pretest, posttest, dan N-gain ternormalisasi disajikan pada Tabel 4.5."
    ))
    pdf5.ln(3)
    pdf5.draw_3line_table(
        "Tabel 4.5 Hasil Pengukuran Pretest, Posttest, dan N-Gain",
        ["Tahap Pengukuran", "N", "Nilai Minimum", "Nilai Maksimum", "Rata-rata", "Std Dev"],
        [
            ["Pretest", "32", "38,00", "65,00", "51,25", "6,80"],
            ["Posttest", "32", "72,00", "96,00", "84,50", "5,40"],
            ["N-Gain Score", "32", "0,45", "0,88", "0,68", "0,11"]
        ],
        [45, 18, 32, 32, 30, 25]
    )
    pdf5.set_font("Helvetica", "", 10)
    # CLEAN ACCURATE REPORTING
    pdf5.multi_cell(0, 5.5, (
        "Berdasarkan Tabel 4.5, nilai pretest siswa memiliki nilai minimum 38,00 dan nilai maksimum 65,00 "
        "dengan rata-rata 51,25. Setelah implementasi model STEM, rata-rata nilai posttest meningkat menjadi 84,50 "
        "dengan rata-rata N-Gain sebesar 0,68 yang tergolong dalam kategori efektivitas sedang."
    ))
    pdf5.output(os.path.join(target_dir, "DOC-SKRIPSI-EDU-05_Eksperimen_Pembelajaran_STEM.pdf"))

    # 6. Statistika Uji Asumsi Klasik (Prerequisite Logic Inversion)
    pdf6 = AcademicPdf("Statistika: Uji Asumsi Klasik Regresi")
    pdf6.add_page()
    pdf6.set_font("Helvetica", "B", 13)
    pdf6.cell(0, 8, "BAB IV: UJI ASUMSI KLASIK DAN REGRESI", 0, 1, "C")
    pdf6.ln(2)
    pdf6.set_font("Helvetica", "B", 11)
    pdf6.cell(0, 6, "4.1 Uji Normalitas dan Homogenitas Varians", 0, 1, "L")
    pdf6.set_font("Helvetica", "", 10)
    pdf6.multi_cell(0, 5.5, (
        "Sebelum dilakukan uji regresi linear berganda, seluruh data residual model harus diuji "
        "apakah memenuhi asumsi normalitas melalui uji Kolmogorov-Smirnov."
    ))
    pdf6.ln(3)
    pdf6.draw_3line_table(
        "Tabel 4.6 Hasil Uji Normalitas One-Sample Kolmogorov-Smirnov",
        ["Parameter Uji", "Residual Model Regresi"],
        [
            ["N Sampel", "80"],
            ["Kolmogorov-Smirnov Z", "1,85"],
            ["Asymp. Sig. (2-tailed)", "0,012"]
        ],
        [90, 90]
    )
    pdf6.set_font("Helvetica", "", 10)
    # INJECTED INVERSION ERROR: Sig = 0.012 claimed as NORMAL
    pdf6.multi_cell(0, 5.5, (
        "Berdasarkan hasil uji Kolmogorov-Smirnov pada Tabel 4.6, diperoleh nilai Asymp. Sig. sebesar 0,012. "
        "Oleh karena nilai signifikansi lebih kecil dari 0,05, peneliti menyimpulkan bahwa data terdistribusi secara normal "
        "dan asumsi klasik normalitas terpenuhi."
    ))
    pdf6.output(os.path.join(target_dir, "DOC-JURNAL-STAT-06_Uji_Asumsi_Regresi.pdf"))

    # 7. Data Science Clustering & Segmentasi
    pdf7 = AcademicPdf("Data Science: Evaluasi Segmentasi Pelanggan")
    pdf7.add_page()
    pdf7.set_font("Helvetica", "B", 13)
    pdf7.cell(0, 8, "BAB IV: HASIL CLUSTERING DAN SEGMENTASI", 0, 1, "C")
    pdf7.ln(2)
    pdf7.set_font("Helvetica", "B", 11)
    pdf7.cell(0, 6, "4.3 Evaluasi Kualitas Partisi Klaster", 0, 1, "L")
    pdf7.set_font("Helvetica", "", 10)
    pdf7.multi_cell(0, 5.5, (
        "Evaluasi kualitas pemisahan klaster dilakukan dengan membandingkan metrik Silhouette Score, "
        "Davies-Bouldin Index (DBI), dan Calinski-Harabasz Index."
    ))
    pdf7.ln(3)
    pdf7.draw_3line_table(
        "Tabel 4.7 Evaluasi Metrik Kualitas Klaster K-Means (k=3 s.d. k=5)",
        ["Jumlah Klaster (k)", "Silhouette Score", "Davies-Bouldin Index", "Calinski-Harabasz"],
        [
            ["k = 3", "0,68", "0,54", "1420,5"],
            ["k = 4", "0,52", "0,82", "1180,2"],
            ["k = 5", "0,44", "1,15", "950,8"]
        ],
        [45, 45, 50, 40]
    )
    pdf7.set_font("Helvetica", "", 10)
    # INJECTED POLARITY ERROR on DBI: higher DBI claimed as best
    pdf7.multi_cell(0, 5.5, (
        "Berdasarkan Tabel 4.7, konfigurasi k = 3 menghasilkan nilai Silhouette Score tertinggi sebesar 0,68. "
        "Namun peneliti memilih konfigurasi k = 5 sebagai model klaster terbaik karena menghasilkan "
        "nilai Davies-Bouldin Index tertinggi sebesar 1,15."
    ))
    pdf7.output(os.path.join(target_dir, "DOC-SKRIPSI-DS-07_Segmentasi_Clustering.pdf"))

    # 8. Teknik Industri Pengendalian Kualitas
    pdf8 = AcademicPdf("Teknik Industri: Kapabilitas Proses")
    pdf8.add_page()
    pdf8.set_font("Helvetica", "B", 13)
    pdf8.cell(0, 8, "BAB IV: ANALISIS KAPABILITAS PROSES PRODUKSI", 0, 1, "C")
    pdf8.ln(2)
    pdf8.set_font("Helvetica", "B", 11)
    pdf8.cell(0, 6, "4.2 Indeks Kapabilitas Mesin Milling CNC", 0, 1, "L")
    pdf8.set_font("Helvetica", "", 10)
    pdf8.multi_cell(0, 5.5, (
        "Analisis pengendalian mutu dimensi poros baja dilakukan pada 50 sampel pengukuran toleransi (N=50). "
        "Nilai statistik deskriptif dan indeks kapabilitas disajikan pada Tabel 4.8."
    ))
    pdf8.ln(3)
    pdf8.draw_3line_table(
        "Tabel 4.8 Parameter Kapabilitas Proses Poros Baja (N=50)",
        ["Parameter", "Nilai", "Batas Bawah", "Batas Atas"],
        [
            ["Mean Diameter (mm)", "25,02", "24,95", "25,05"],
            ["Standar Deviasi", "0,018", "-", "-"],
            ["Indeks Cp", "1,48", "1,33", "-"],
            ["Indeks Cpk", "1,35", "1,33", "-"]
        ],
        [60, 40, 40, 40]
    )
    pdf8.set_font("Helvetica", "", 10)
    # CLEAN REPORTING
    pdf8.multi_cell(0, 5.5, (
        "Berdasarkan Tabel 4.8, rata-rata diameter poros baja tercatat sebesar 25,02 mm dengan standar deviasi "
        "sebesar 0,018. Nilai indeks kapabilitas proses Cp sebesar 1,48 dan Cpk sebesar 1,35 berada di atas standar "
        "minimum 1,33 yang menandakan proses produksi berada dalam kondisi terkendali dan kapabel."
    ))
    pdf8.output(os.path.join(target_dir, "DOC-SKRIPSI-TIN-08_Kapabilitas_Proses.pdf"))

    # 9. AI Computer Vision Deteksi Objek
    pdf9 = AcademicPdf("AI: Deteksi Objek Real-Time")
    pdf9.add_page()
    pdf9.set_font("Helvetica", "B", 13)
    pdf9.cell(0, 8, "BAB IV: PERFORMA DETEKSI OBJEK CITRA DRONE", 0, 1, "C")
    pdf9.ln(2)
    pdf9.set_font("Helvetica", "B", 11)
    pdf9.cell(0, 6, "4.4 Perbandingan Model YOLOv8 vs RT-DETR", 0, 1, "L")
    pdf9.set_font("Helvetica", "", 10)
    pdf9.multi_cell(0, 5.5, (
        "Pengujian komparatif model deteksi objek kendaraan pada citra udara dievaluasi pada resolusi 640x640 "
        "menggunakan metrik mAP50, mAP50-95, dan Latensi Inferensi."
    ))
    pdf9.ln(3)
    pdf9.draw_3line_table(
        "Tabel 4.9 Perbandingan Performa Arsitektur Detektor Objek",
        ["Model Arsitektur", "mAP@50", "mAP@50-95", "Latensi (ms)", "Throughput (FPS)"],
        [
            ["YOLOv8-Small", "0,845", "0,612", "12,4", "80,6"],
            ["YOLOv8-Medium", "0,892", "0,684", "24,8", "40,3"],
            ["RT-DETR-Base", "0,875", "0,650", "35,2", "28,4"]
        ],
        [45, 32, 35, 35, 35]
    )
    pdf9.set_font("Helvetica", "", 10)
    # INJECTED ROUNDING TRUNCATION: mAP50 0.845 rounds to 0.85, written 0.84
    pdf9.multi_cell(0, 5.5, (
        "Berdasarkan Tabel 4.9, model YOLOv8-Small menghasilkan nilai mAP@50 sebesar 0,84 dengan latensi "
        "sebesar 12,4 ms. Model YOLOv8-Medium mencatatkan tingkat akurasi tertinggi dengan mAP@50 sebesar 0,89 "
        "dan mAP@50-95 sebesar 0,68."
    ))
    pdf9.output(os.path.join(target_dir, "DOC-JURNAL-AI-09_Deteksi_Objek_Vision.pdf"))

    # 10. Dokumen Kontrol 100% Bersih (True Negative)
    pdf10 = AcademicPdf("Publikasi: Validasi Eksperimen Terkendali")
    pdf10.add_page()
    pdf10.set_font("Helvetica", "B", 13)
    pdf10.cell(0, 8, "HASIL DAN PEMBAHASAN EKSPERIMEN", 0, 1, "C")
    pdf10.ln(2)
    pdf10.set_font("Helvetica", "B", 11)
    pdf10.cell(0, 6, "Hasil Pengujian Akurasi Klasifikasi Multikelas", 0, 1, "L")
    pdf10.set_font("Helvetica", "", 10)
    pdf10.multi_cell(0, 5.5, (
        "Eksperimen klasifikasi multikelas pada 100 sampel uji independen menghasilkan matriks evaluasi "
        "yang sepenuhnya konsisten antar-kolom metrik dan narasi kesimpulan."
    ))
    pdf10.ln(3)
    pdf10.draw_3line_table(
        "Tabel 1 Evaluasi Kinerja Klasifikasi Multikelas Terverifikasi",
        ["Kelas", "Presisi", "Recall", "F1-Score", "Jumlah Sampel"],
        [
            ["Kelas A", "0,92", "0,90", "0,91", "30"],
            ["Kelas B", "0,88", "0,85", "0,86", "35"],
            ["Kelas C", "0,95", "0,94", "0,95", "35"]
        ],
        [40, 35, 35, 35, 35]
    )
    pdf10.set_font("Helvetica", "", 10)
    # 100% CLEAN CONTROL
    pdf10.multi_cell(0, 5.5, (
        "Berdasarkan Tabel 1, Kelas A memperoleh nilai presisi 0,92, recall 0,90, dan F1-Score 0,91 "
        "pada 30 sampel. Kelas C mencatatkan performa klasifikasi tertinggi dengan presisi 0,95 dan recall 0,94. "
        "Seluruh hasil pengujian berada dalam rentang toleransi validitas ilmiah tanpa inkonsistensi numerik."
    ))
    pdf10.output(os.path.join(target_dir, "DOC-KONTROL-10_Naskah_Bebas_Galat_100.pdf"))

    print(f"Berhasil menghasilkan 10 naskah PDF akademik baru di: {target_dir}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    out_dir = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))), "data", "uploads")
    generate_all_expanded_pdfs(out_dir)

# FILE [23/58]: data/presets/generate_presets.py
# UKURAN: 7.6 KB | JUMLAH BARIS: 220 | PERAN: Generator script creating precomputed preset test cases for live demonstrations.
################################################################################
"""
Helper script to generate the 3 Golden Preset PDFs for LOCUS.
Uses fpdf2 (installed) to produce clean, realistic thesis/journal draft pages.
"""

import os
from fpdf import FPDF


class PresetPdf(FPDF):
    def header(self):
        self.set_font("Helvetica", "I", 9)
        self.set_text_color(128, 128, 128)
        self.cell(0, 10, "LOCUS Academic Audit Benchmark Dataset -- Draft Publikasi Ilmiah", 0, 1, "R")
        self.ln(2)

    def footer(self):
        self.set_y(-15)
        self.set_font("Helvetica", "I", 9)
        self.set_text_color(128, 128, 128)
        self.cell(0, 10, f"Halaman {self.page_no()}", 0, 0, "C")


def create_preset_1(output_path: str):
    pdf = PresetPdf()
    pdf.add_page()
    pdf.set_auto_page_break(auto=True, margin=15)

    pdf.set_font("Helvetica", "B", 14)
    pdf.set_text_color(20, 20, 20)
    pdf.cell(0, 10, "BAB IV: HASIL DAN PEMBAHASAN", 0, 1, "C")
    pdf.ln(4)

    pdf.set_font("Helvetica", "B", 11)
    pdf.cell(0, 8, "4.3 Evaluasi Kinerja Model Prediksi", 0, 1, "L")

    pdf.set_font("Helvetica", "", 10)
    pdf.multi_cell(0, 6, (
        "Berdasarkan pengujian komparatif terhadap dataset uji, metrik evaluasi Root Mean Squared Error (RMSE), "
        "Mean Absolute Error (MAE), dan Akurasi dari ketiga algoritma pembelajaran mesin disajikan pada Tabel 4.3 berikut:"
    ))
    pdf.ln(4)

    # Table 4.3 (3-line scientific table)
    pdf.set_font("Helvetica", "B", 10)
    pdf.cell(0, 6, "Tabel 4.3 Perbandingan Performa Model Prediksi", 0, 1, "L")

    # Table Top Line
    pdf.set_line_width(0.6)
    pdf.line(pdf.get_x(), pdf.get_y(), pdf.get_x() + 180, pdf.get_y())

    # Header Row
    pdf.set_font("Helvetica", "B", 9)
    col_w = [50, 40, 40, 50]
    headers = ["Model Algoritma", "RMSE", "MAE", "Akurasi"]
    for i, h in enumerate(headers):
        pdf.cell(col_w[i], 8, h, 0, 0, "C" if i > 0 else "L")
    pdf.ln(8)

    # Sub-header Line
    pdf.set_line_width(0.3)
    pdf.line(pdf.get_x(), pdf.get_y(), pdf.get_x() + 180, pdf.get_y())

    # Data Rows
    pdf.set_font("Helvetica", "", 9)
    rows = [
        ["Random Forest", "0,92", "0,78", "0,84"],
        ["XGBoost", "0,41", "0,35", "0,91"],
        ["Support Vector Machine", "0,57", "0,49", "0,86"]
    ]
    for r in rows:
        for i, val in enumerate(r):
            pdf.cell(col_w[i], 7, val, 0, 0, "C" if i > 0 else "L")
        pdf.ln(7)

    # Bottom Line
    pdf.set_line_width(0.6)
    pdf.line(pdf.get_x(), pdf.get_y(), pdf.get_x() + 180, pdf.get_y())
    pdf.ln(6)

    # Narration Claim with Polarity Error
    pdf.set_font("Helvetica", "", 10)
    claim_text = (
        "Berdasarkan Tabel 4.3 di atas, Model Random Forest memiliki kinerja prediksi terbaik "
        "karena menghasilkan nilai RMSE tertinggi sebesar 0,92 dibandingkan model lainnya. "
        "Hasil ini membuktikan bahwa arsitektur ensemble pohon keputusan mampu meminimalkan galat estimasi secara optimal."
    )
    pdf.multi_cell(0, 6, claim_text)
    pdf.output(output_path)


def create_preset_2(output_path: str):
    pdf = PresetPdf()
    pdf.add_page()
    pdf.set_auto_page_break(auto=True, margin=15)

    pdf.set_font("Helvetica", "B", 14)
    pdf.cell(0, 10, "BAB IV: ANALISIS DATA PENELITIAN", 0, 1, "C")
    pdf.ln(4)

    pdf.set_font("Helvetica", "B", 11)
    pdf.cell(0, 8, "4.2 Uji Prasyarat Analisis Data", 0, 1, "L")

    pdf.set_font("Helvetica", "", 10)
    pdf.multi_cell(0, 6, (
        "Sebelum dilakukan pengujian hipotesis regresi linear, dilakukan uji normalitas data untuk memenuhi asumsi klasik. "
        "Uji normalitas dilakukan menggunakan uji Kolmogorov-Smirnov dan Shapiro-Wilk pada taraf signifikansi alpha = 0,05."
    ))
    pdf.ln(4)

    # Table 4.1
    pdf.set_font("Helvetica", "B", 10)
    pdf.cell(0, 6, "Tabel 4.1 Hasil Uji Normalitas Kolmogorov-Smirnov dan Shapiro-Wilk", 0, 1, "L")

    pdf.set_line_width(0.6)
    pdf.line(pdf.get_x(), pdf.get_y(), pdf.get_x() + 180, pdf.get_y())

    pdf.set_font("Helvetica", "B", 9)
    col_w = [60, 60, 60]
    headers = ["Variabel Penelitian", "Kolmogorov-Smirnov (Sig.)", "Shapiro-Wilk (Sig.)"]
    for i, h in enumerate(headers):
        pdf.cell(col_w[i], 8, h, 0, 0, "C" if i > 0 else "L")
    pdf.ln(8)

    pdf.set_line_width(0.3)
    pdf.line(pdf.get_x(), pdf.get_y(), pdf.get_x() + 180, pdf.get_y())

    pdf.set_font("Helvetica", "", 9)
    rows = [
        ["Kecemasan Akademik (Y)", "0,018", "0,012"],
        ["Efikasi Diri (X)", "0,064", "0,071"]
    ]
    for r in rows:
        for i, val in enumerate(r):
            pdf.cell(col_w[i], 7, val, 0, 0, "C" if i > 0 else "L")
        pdf.ln(7)

    pdf.set_line_width(0.6)
    pdf.line(pdf.get_x(), pdf.get_y(), pdf.get_x() + 180, pdf.get_y())
    pdf.ln(6)

    # Claim with Inversion error
    pdf.set_font("Helvetica", "", 10)
    claim_text = (
        "Sebagaimana tertera pada Tabel 4.1, data penelitian berdistribusi normal karena nilai Sig. "
        "pada uji Kolmogorov-Smirnov untuk variabel Kecemasan Akademik diperoleh sebesar 0,018 < 0,05. "
        "Oleh karena itu, pengujian statistik parametrik dapat dilanjutkan tanpa transformasi data."
    )
    pdf.multi_cell(0, 6, claim_text)
    pdf.output(output_path)


def create_preset_3(output_path: str):
    pdf = PresetPdf()
    pdf.add_page()
    pdf.set_auto_page_break(auto=True, margin=15)

    pdf.set_font("Helvetica", "B", 14)
    pdf.cell(0, 10, "BAB IV: HASIL EKSPERIMEN DAN PEMBAHASAN", 0, 1, "C")
    pdf.ln(4)

    pdf.set_font("Helvetica", "B", 11)
    pdf.cell(0, 8, "4.4 Pengujian Hipotesis Efektivitas Pembelajaran", 0, 1, "L")

    pdf.set_font("Helvetica", "", 10)
    pdf.multi_cell(0, 6, (
        "Untuk menguji signifikansi perbedaan peningkatan kemampuan siswa antara kelompok kontrol dan eksperimen, "
        "dilakukan uji Independent Sample t-test dengan derajat bebas df = 58."
    ))
    pdf.ln(4)

    # Table 4.5
    pdf.set_font("Helvetica", "B", 10)
    pdf.cell(0, 6, "Tabel 4.5 Hasil Uji-t Perbedaan Skor Kelompok Kontrol dan Eksperimen", 0, 1, "L")

    pdf.set_line_width(0.6)
    pdf.line(pdf.get_x(), pdf.get_y(), pdf.get_x() + 180, pdf.get_y())

    pdf.set_font("Helvetica", "B", 9)
    col_w = [45, 25, 35, 35, 40]
    headers = ["Kelompok", "N", "Rata-rata Pretest", "Rata-rata Posttest", "Uji-t (df=58)"]
    for i, h in enumerate(headers):
        pdf.cell(col_w[i], 8, h, 0, 0, "C" if i > 0 else "L")
    pdf.ln(8)

    pdf.set_line_width(0.3)
    pdf.line(pdf.get_x(), pdf.get_y(), pdf.get_x() + 180, pdf.get_y())

    pdf.set_font("Helvetica", "", 9)
    rows = [
        ["Kelompok Kontrol", "30", "65,40", "68,20", "t = 1,12 (p = 0,267)"],
        ["Kelompok Eksperimen", "30", "64,80", "78,50", "t = 2,41 (p = 0,002)"]
    ]
    for r in rows:
        for i, val in enumerate(r):
            pdf.cell(col_w[i], 7, val, 0, 0, "C" if i > 0 else "L")
        pdf.ln(7)

    pdf.set_line_width(0.6)
    pdf.line(pdf.get_x(), pdf.get_y(), pdf.get_x() + 180, pdf.get_y())
    pdf.ln(6)

    # Claim with Statcheck inconsistency
    pdf.set_font("Helvetica", "", 10)
    claim_text = (
        "Berdasarkan Tabel 4.5, penerapan media pembelajaran inovatif berpengaruh signifikan "
        "terhadap hasil belajar siswa dengan nilai t(58) = 2,41 dan nilai signifikansi p = 0,002. "
        "Peningkatan ini membuktikan hipotesis tindakan diterima secara meyakinkan."
    )
    pdf.multi_cell(0, 6, claim_text)
    pdf.output(output_path)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    out_dir = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
    os.makedirs(out_dir, exist_ok=True)
    create_preset_1(os.path.join(out_dir, "preset_1_ml_polarity.pdf"))
    create_preset_2(os.path.join(out_dir, "preset_2_normality_inversion.pdf"))
    create_preset_3(os.path.join(out_dir, "preset_3_statcheck_grim.pdf"))
    print("Presets created successfully.")

# [SELESAI FILE: data/presets/generate_presets.py]

# FILE [25/58]: desktop_launcher.py
# UKURAN: 2.8 KB | JUMLAH BARIS: 105 | PERAN: Desktop application launcher wrapping local FastAPI and native webview.
################################################################################
"""
LOCUS (Cursor for Scientific Integrity)
Desktop Application Launcher (PyInstaller & pywebview Wrapper)
Mode: 100% Offline (127.0.0.1:8000)
"""

import sys
import os

# Force 100% Offline mode for Hugging Face and ML libraries
os.environ.setdefault("HF_HUB_OFFLINE", "1")
os.environ.setdefault("TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE", "1")
os.environ.setdefault("HF_DATASETS_OFFLINE", "1")
os.environ.setdefault("TOKENIZERS_PARALLELISM", "false")
import time
import threading
import uvicorn
import urllib.request
import webbrowser

# Add current workspace directory to sys.path
BASE_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
if BASE_DIR not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, BASE_DIR)

from backend.main import app

HOST = "127.0.0.1"
PORT = 8000
SERVER_URL = f"http://{HOST}:{PORT}"


def start_server():
    """Runs uvicorn server in a dedicated thread."""
    config = uvicorn.Config(
        app=app,
        host=HOST,
        port=PORT,
        log_level="warning",
        access_log=False
    )
    server = uvicorn.Server(config)
    server.run()


def wait_for_server(timeout_sec=15):
    """Waits until the FastAPI local server is reachable."""
    start = time.time()
    while time.time() - start < timeout_sec:
        try:
            with urllib.request.urlopen(f"{SERVER_URL}/", timeout=1.0) as resp:
                if resp.status == 200:
                    return True
        except Exception:
            time.sleep(0.3)
    return False


def main():
    print("=" * 60)
    print("  LOCUS v3.1: Cursor for Scientific Integrity")
    print(f"  Mode: 100% Offline | Alamat: {SERVER_URL}")
    print("=" * 60)

    # 1. Start server in daemon thread
    server_thread = threading.Thread(target=start_server, daemon=True)
    server_thread.start()

    # 2. Wait for server readiness
    print("Memulai server audit neuro-simbolik lokal...")
    time.sleep(1.0)

    # 3. Check pywebview for desktop native shell
    has_webview = False
    try:
        import webview
        has_webview = True
    except ImportError:
        has_webview = False

    if has_webview:
        print("Membuka jendela aplikasi desktop native (pywebview)...")
        window = webview.create_window(
            title="LOCUS: Cursor for Scientific Integrity",
            url=SERVER_URL,
            width=1366,
            height=860,
            min_size=(960, 640),
            text_select=True
        )
        webview.start()
    else:
        print("Membuka antarmuka melalui peramban web bawaan...")
        webbrowser.open(SERVER_URL)
        print("Tekan Ctrl+C di terminal ini untuk menghentikan aplikasi.")
        try:
            while True:
                time.sleep(1)
        except KeyboardInterrupt:
            print("\nMenghentikan LOCUS. Sampai jumpa!")
            sys.exit(0)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

# [SELESAI FILE: desktop_launcher.py]

# FILE [26/58]: docs/PRD_UI_GOOGLE_STITCH.md
# UKURAN: 18.6 KB | JUMLAH BARIS: 273 | PERAN: UI design specification and interaction model for LOCUS Studio dual-pane layout.
################################################################################
# Product Requirement Document (PRD) - UI/UX LOCUS
## Cursor for Scientific Integrity (Google Stitch Ready)

---

## 1. Executive Summary & Objective

* **Product Name:** LOCUS (/ LO-KUS /)
* **Tagline:** Offline Neuro-Symbolic Proof Engine & Dual-Pane Auditor for Scientific Integrity.
* **Target Users:** 
  1. Dosen Penguji & Pembimbing Skripsi / Tesis
  2. Juri Hackathon & Evaluator Ilmiah
  3. Mahasiswa & Peneliti Akademik
  4. Reviewer Jurnal & Editor Ilmiah
* **Core Value Proposition:** Menemukan inkonsistensi antara teks narasi klaim dan tabel empiris dalam skripsi/jurnal ilmiah dalam **di bawah 3 detik**, diverifikasi secara deterministik menggunakan solver matematika (SciPy), bebas halusinasi AI (0% false positive), dan beroperasi 100% lokal/offline (Zero-PII).
* **Goal of this PRD:** Memberikan spesifikasi visual, hierarki tata letak, kamus token desain, spesifikasi komponen mikro, serta template prompt terstruktur untuk di-generate langsung ke dalam **Google Stitch**.

---

## 2. Design System & Anti-Slop Philosophy

LOCUS mengadopsi prinsip desain **Pure Notion-Warm Editorial Minimalist**. Menghindari tampilan generic AI-slop (seperti gradien ungu neon berlebihan, kartu melayang tanpa batas tegas, atau emoji kekanak-kanakan). Tampilan harus memancarkan ketelitian, keanggunan akademis, dan keterbacaan tinggi.

### 2.1 Color Palette Tokens

| Token Name | Hex Code | Deskripsi & Penggunaan |
|---|---|---|
| `--color-paper-warmth` | `#f6f5f4` | Background utama kanvas dan halaman kerja (Notion warm paper). |
| `--color-pure-white` | `#ffffff` | Background kartu temuan, modal, dan container inspeksi. |
| `--color-ink-black` | `#111827` | Teks utama, judul, dan angka metrik penting (WCAG AAA). |
| `--color-graphite` | `#374151` | Teks sekunder, deskripsi penjelasan, dan sub-judul. |
| `--color-stone` | `#6b7280` | Metadata, nomor halaman, caption, dan border halus. |
| `--color-hairline` | `rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.08)` | Garis batas struktural kanvas dan kartu. |
| `--color-notion-blue` | `#0075de` | Aksen primer, tombol navigasi antar-halaman, dan link rujukan. |
| `--color-rose-claim` | `#e11d48` | Sorotan teks klaim bermasalah (Border: `#e11d48`, Wash: `rgba(225,29,72,0.08)`). |
| `--color-amber-evidence`| `#d97706` | Sorotan sel tabel bukti (Border: `#d97706`, Wash: `rgba(245,158,11,0.12)`). |
| `--color-emerald-pass` | `#10b981` | Status konsisten / verifikasi valid (Border: `#10b981`, Wash: `rgba(16,185,129,0.10)`). |

### 2.2 Typography Stack

* **Heading & Body Font:** Inter / -apple-system / BlinkMacSystemFont / "Segoe UI" (Sans-serif bersih, netral, berbobot 400, 500, 600, 700).
* **Mathematical & Data Font:** JetBrains Mono / SF Mono / Consolas (Monospace berjarak tetap untuk koordinat sel, angka perbandingan diff, dan formula SciPy).
* **Ukuran Tipografi:**
  - Hero Title: 32px / Line-height 1.25 / Bold 700
  - Section Header: 18px / Line-height 1.35 / Bold 700
  - Finding Card Title: 14px / Semi-bold 600
  - Diff Box Numbers: 15px / Monospace / Bold 700
  - Body Text: 13px / Line-height 1.6 / Regular 400
  - Micro Metadata: 10px-11px / Medium 500 / Letter-spacing +0.3px

### 2.3 Visual Rhythm & Spacing

* Grid: 8pt baseline grid.
* Card Corner Radius: `6px` (bukan pill bundar berlebihan, mempertahankan sudut tajam dokumen akademis).
* Shadow System: Elevasi sangat halus (`0 1px 3px rgba(0,0,0,0.04)`). Tidak ada drop shadow buram 3D.

---

## 3. Information Architecture & Screen Breakdown

LOCUS terdiri dari 2 layar utama dan 1 modal pelengkap:

```
[ SCREEN 1: LANDING & ONBOARDING ]
               │
   (Unggah PDF / Pilih Preset)
               ▼
[ SCREEN 2: DUAL-PANE AUDIT WORKSPACE ]
  ├── Panel Kiri (55%): X-Ray PDF Canvas + BBox Segments + Editorial Gutter Bracket
  └── Panel Kanan (45%): Sticky Filter Bar + 3-Second Format Cards + Mini Row Snippets
               │
   (Klik Ekspor Bukti)
               ▼
[ MODAL 3: CRYPTOGRAPHIC VERIFICATION RECEIPT ]
```

---

## 4. Spesifikasi Detil Layar (Screen by Screen)

### Layar 1: Landing, Showcase Solvers & Upload Onboarding

#### Header / Navigation Bar
* **Kiri:** Logo tipografis `LOCUS` bersanding dengan subtitle kecil `Cursor for Scientific Integrity`.
* **Kanan:** Indikator status mesin (`Local Engine Active · 100% Offline`), tombol dokumentasi, dan tautan GitHub/Arkavidia.

#### Hero Section
* **Academic Kicker Strip:** Label ringkas di atas judul: `STANDAR VERIFIKASI AKADEMIK · ARKAVヌDIA 11.0 HMIF ITB`.
* **Headline:** "Audit Integritas Data Skripsi & Makalah Ilmiah dalam Hitungan Detik."
* **Sub-headline:** "Neuro-symbolic proof engine yang secara otomatis menautkan klaim kalimat ke tabel empiris, memverifikasi konsistensi matematika murni, dan menjamin 0% false positive."
* **Trust Badges Strip:**
  - 8 Mesin Solver Deterministik (Statcheck, GRIM, GRIMMER, DEBIT, Steeper Total-N, Confusion Matrix, Tuple Verifier, SymPy Formula).
  - Garansi 0% False Positive (Gerbang Abstensi Tau-Link >= 0.75).
  - 100% Offline & Zero-PII (Data tidak pernah keluar dari memori lokal komputer).

#### Interactive Golden Presets Bar (Untuk Pengujian Panggung / Juri)
Tiga tombol cepat untuk memuat kasus uji nyata tanpa harus mencari berkas PDF:
1. **Preset 1:** *Evaluasi Model ML* (Inversi Polaritas Metrik Galat RMSE vs Akurasi).
2. **Preset 2:** *Uji Prasyarat Normalitas* (Jebakan Sidang: Nilai Sig. < 0,05 disimpulkan normal).
3. **Preset 3:** *Uji-t Eksperimen* (Pembalikan Keputusan Signifikansi Statcheck p=0,002 vs p=0,019).

#### Upload Dropzone
* Area drag-and-drop dokumen PDF bergaris putus-putus elegan (`border: 1.5px dashed rgba(0,0,0,0.15)`).
* Pesan instruksi: "Tarik & letakkan berkas PDF skripsi (Bab 4/Bab 3) atau klik untuk memilih berkas".
* Indikator kompatibilitas: "Mendukung format naskah skripsi Indonesia, SPSS, R, dan format publikasi IEEE/APA".

---

### Layar 2: Dual-Pane Audit Workspace (Antarmuka Inti)

Layar ini menggunakan pembagian layar terbelah (*split canvas*) responsif:

```
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  TOP TOOLBAR: Judul Dokumen · Paginasi [ < 3/18 > ] · Zoom [ - 100% + ] · Tombol Ekspor      │
├──────────────────────────────────────────────┼──────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│  PANEL KIRI (55%): PDF X-RAY CANVAS          │  PANEL KANAN (45%): EVIDENCE INSPECTOR       │
│                                              │                                              │
│  [Gutter] [Teks Halaman PDF Asli]            │  [ Sticky Filter: Semua | Beda Tabel | Ralat ]│
│     │                                        │                                              │
│     ├── Kotak Klaim Merah Muda (Line-by-Line)│  ┌── KARTU TEMUAN 3-DETIK ─────────────────┐  │
│     │   (Menyorot kalimat yang diuji)        │  │ [Badge Status] Salah Pembulatan Angka   │  │
│     │                                        │  │                                         │  │
│     └── Bracket Gutter Margin Kiri           │  │ ┌── KOTAK PERBANDINGAN BERDAMPINGAN ──┐ │  │
│         (Jalur garis rapi tanpa motong teks) │  │ │ Tertulis di Teks: │ Tertera di Tabel:│ │  │
│                                              │  │ │     2,67          ➔    2.6759       │ │  │
│     ┌──────────────────────────────────┐     │  │ │                   │  (Baku: 2,68)   │ │  │
│     │ TABEL BUKTI EMPIRIS              │     │  │ └───────────────────────────────────┘ │  │
│     │ [Baris SQRTPGLMN]                │     │  │ Penjelasan: Penulis memotong desimal... │  │
│     │ [Sel Mean: 2.6759...] (Kuning)   │     │  ├─────────────────────────────────────────┤  │
│     └──────────────────────────────────┘     │  │ ▦ Cuplikan Baris Tabel Bukti (Hal. 2)   │  │
│                                              │  │ [DELAY: 76.4 | SQRTPGLMN: 2.6759* | ..] │  │
│                                              │  │ [Tombol: Lompat ke Tabel (Hal. 2) ↗]    │  │
│                                              │  ├─────────────────────────────────────────┤  │
│                                              │  │ Kutipan Kalimat Teks Mahasiswa          │  │
│                                              │  │ Bukti Matematika SciPy & What-If Live   │  │
│                                              │  │ Kuis Pembelajaran (Pedagogical MCQ)     │  │
│                                              │  └─────────────────────────────────────────┘  │
└──────────────────────────────────────────────┴──────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

#### Spesifikasi Panel Kiri: High-Precision PDF X-Ray Canvas
1. **Interactive Multi-Line Highlight (`xray-line-segment`):**
   - Kotak highlight merah muda tidak membungkus blok persegi besar yang meluap ke kalimat lain.
   - Menggunakan highlight segmental per baris teks kalimat yang diuji (`rgba(225, 29, 72, 0.12)` dengan border `1.5px solid #e11d48`).
2. **Table Evidence Cell Pins:**
   - Sel tabel yang menjadi rujukan ditandai dengan kotak kuning ambar (`rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.14)` dengan border `1.5px solid #d97706`).
   - Interior sel tetap 100% transparan agar angka asli tabel di PDF tetap terbaca jelas oleh mata penguji.
3. **Editorial Margin Gutter Bracket (Bukan Garis Silang):**
   - Jalur penghubung antara klaim narasi dan sel tabel ditarik melalui gutter margin kiri (`x = 14px`) menggunakan kurva lengkung kubik `M ... C ...`. Garis **tidak pernah memotong teks dokumen** sehingga estetika kertas skripsi tetap terjaga.

#### Spesifikasi Panel Kanan: Kartu Temuan "Mudah Dibaca dalam 3 Detik"
Setiap kartu temuan diwajibkan menyajikan hirarki informasi dengan urutan berikut:

1. **Header Kartu:**
   - Judul Bahasa Indonesia Ramah Awam (Huruf tebal 14px):
     * *Salah Pembulatan Angka* (menggantikan `WARN_TRUNCATION_ROUNDING`)
     * *Angka Berbeda dengan Tabel* (menggantikan `DIRECT_CELL_CONTRADICTION`)
     * *Salah Ketik Digit (Transposisi)* (menggantikan `DIGIT_TRANSPOSITION_TYPO`)
     * *Logika Uji Prasyarat Terbalik* (menggantikan `PREREQUISITE_LOGIC_INVERSION`)
     * *Pembalikan Keputusan Signifikansi* (menggantikan `DECISION_FLIP`)
   - Badge kode teknis kecil di samping (`F-C_3_21-TUPLE · Hal. 3`).

2. **Diff 3-Second Format Box (Wajib di Paling Atas):**
   - **Kolom Kiri (Aksen Merah Muda):**
     * Label: `Tertulis di Kalimat (Hal. X):`
     * Angka: Nilai yang ditulis mahasiswa dalam teks narasi (contoh: `2,67`).
   - **Ikon Panah Tengah:** `➔`
   - **Kolom Kanan (Aksen Kuning Ambar):**
     * Label: `Tertera di Tabel (Hal. Y):`
     * Angka: Nilai eksak sel tabel asli (contoh: `2.67593335`) + Badge Pembulatan Baku `(Baku: 2,68)`.
   - **Callout Penjelasan Singkat (1 Kalimat):**
     * Strip latar belakang biru muda dengan border kiri biru: "Penulis memotong desimal angka menjadi 2,67, padahal aturan pembulatan baku matematika seharusnya dibulatkan ke atas menjadi 2,68."

3. **Cuplikan Baris Tabel Langsung (Mini Table Row Snippet):**
   - Menampilkan mini tabel 1 baris yang langsung memperlihatkan baris variabel terkait beserta seluruh nama kolomnya.
   - Sel target ditandai dengan warna ambar dan badge `Bukti`.
   - Disertai tombol navigasi: `[Lompat ke Tabel (Hal. Y) ↗]` sehingga penguji tidak perlu repot mencari manual halaman tabel.

4. **Kutipan Kalimat Asli:**
   - Kotak kutipan bergaris batas kiri lembut berisi kalimat lengkap mahasiswa yang diperiksa.

5. **Bukti Matematika Deterministik (SciPy Callout):**
   - Menampilkan rumus rekalkulasi baku ilmiah secara rapi (contoh: `SQRTPGLMN.Mean: Sel = 2.67593 -> Pembulatan 2 desimal = 2.68 != 2.67`).

6. **Interactive What-If Live Stepper:**
   - Tombol step `[ − ]` dan `[ + ]` dengan delta `±0.01` untuk mensimulasikan nilai batas kritis di mana temuan berubah status secara live.

7. **Explain-First Pedagogical Scaffolding:**
   - Pertanyaan pilihan ganda mendidik (Multiple Choice Question) untuk menguji pemahaman mahasiswa/peneliti mengapa hal tersebut keliru.
   - Draf usulan kalimat revisi ilmiah yang aman dan siap disalin ke dalam dokumen.

---

### Layar 3: Cryptographic Verification Receipt (Modal Ekspor)

* Ringkasan audit dokumen: Tanggal audit, durasi komputasi (<200 ms), total klaim terperiksa, dan persentase kepatuhan integritas data.
* **Hash Kriptografis SHA-256:** Tanda tangan unik berkas PDF untuk memastikan dokumen tidak dimanipulasi setelah diaudit.
* **Tabel Verifikasi Terperinci:** Daftar seluruh temuan lengkap dengan koordinat bounding box, status verifikasi, dan solver yang memverifikasi.
* **Opsi Redaksi PII (Privasi):** Toggle sakelar untuk menyamarkan nama mahasiswa, NIM, nama dosen pembimbing, dan institusi sebelum tanda terima dicetak/diekspor ke format PDF/JSON.

---

## 5. Structured Prompts untuk Google Stitch

Salin dan gunakan blok prompt berikut saat mendesain antarmuka di **Google Stitch**:

### Prompt Stitch 1: Global Theme & Workspace Layout
```text
Design a clean, professional academic document audit interface inspired by Notion's warm paper aesthetic and modern scientific tools.
Color Palette:
- Canvas Background: #f6f5f4 (warm muted paper)
- Card Surfaces: #ffffff (pure white with 1px border rgba(0,0,0,0.08))
- Primary Accent: #0075de (deep notion blue)
- Error / Contradiction: #e11d48 (rose pink)
- Table Evidence: #d97706 (warm amber)
- Consistent / Pass: #10b981 (emerald green)
- Typography: Inter for UI and JetBrains Mono for numbers, coordinates, and formulas.

Layout Structure:
1. Top Bar: App title "LOCUS", Document name "BAB 4 Skripsi.pdf", Page navigation controls with previous/next buttons, Zoom controls [ - ] [ 100% ] [ + ] [ Fit ], and an "Ekspor Bukti" button.
2. Dual-Pane Workspace:
   - Left Pane (55% width): Centered authentic PDF viewer rendering thesis pages on warm paper with page shadow.
   - Right Pane (45% width): Findings inspector with sticky filter pills ("Semua", "Inkonsistensi Fatal", "Peringatan Pembulatan", "Konsisten").
```

### Prompt Stitch 2: Finding Card Component (3-Second Format)
```text
Design a specialized "Finding Card" for an academic paper audit tool that can be understood by non-technical thesis examiners in under 3 seconds:

Card Elements (Top to Bottom):
1. Card Header:
   - Left: Rounded status tag with a colored status dot and clear Indonesian title: "Salah Pembulatan Angka" (amber) or "Angka Berbeda dengan Tabel" (rose). Small monospace code tag next to it: "WARN_TRUNCATION_ROUNDING".
   - Right: Subtle metadata label "F-C_3_21-TUPLE · Hal. 3".
2. Diff Comparison Box (Prominent at the very top):
   - Two equal columns side-by-side with an arrow (➔) in between:
     * Left Column (Claimed): Label "Tertulis di Kalimat (Hal. 3)" in small caps, with big bold number "2,67" in rose color.
     * Right Column (Table Evidence): Label "Tertera di Tabel (Hal. 2)" in small caps, with bold number "2.6759" in amber color and a pill badge "(Baku: 2,68)".
   - Bottom full-width callout in light blue tint with blue left border: "Penjelasan Singkat: Penulis memotong desimal menjadi 2,67 padahal pembulatan baku matematika seharusnya ke atas menjadi 2,68."
3. Table Row Snippet Preview:
   - Header with table icon, title "Cuplikan Baris Tabel: Tabel 4.2 (Hal. 2)", and a button "Lompat ke Tabel (Hal. 2) ↗".
   - A clean 1-row table preview showing headers (Baris, N, Minimum, Maximum, Mean, Std. Dev) and cells. The Mean cell "2.6759" is highlighted in soft amber with a small "Bukti" badge.
4. Quoted Student Sentence:
   - Elegant blockquote with light grey left border showing the sentence: "Selanjutnya adalah pengalaman auditor (PNGLM)... dengan rata-rata 2,67."
5. SciPy Mathematical Recalculation box with monospace formula.
6. What-If Stepper control [-] 2.6759 [+] with a "Live Re-calc" status badge.
7. Multiple Choice Quiz question with 2 clickable option cards and a hidden recommended revision box.
```

### Prompt Stitch 3: PDF Document Overlay & Evidence Connecting Line
```text
Design the visual overlay layer on top of a rendered academic PDF page:
1. Claim Highlight:
   - A sentence spanning two lines is highlighted using two individual tight rounded rectangle segments wrapping only the text of those two lines, with border 1.5px solid #e11d48 and a soft translucent pink fill rgba(225,29,72,0.12).
   - A small floating tag above the sentence reads: "Klaim Terpilih · Bukti di Hal. 2 ↗".
2. Table Cell Highlight:
   - In a descriptive statistics table, a specific cell under the "Mean" column is outlined with 1.5px solid #d97706 and transparent inner background so the printed numbers remain 100% legible.
3. Editorial Margin Bracket:
   - An elegant bracket line located strictly inside the left document margin (x = 14px) curves gently from the claim sentence level down to the table level, with an arrow pointing toward the table, completely avoiding crossing over any printed document text.
```

---

## 6. Kriteria Keberhasilan Tampilan (Acceptance Criteria)

1. **Kejelasan Waktu Baca (3-Second Rule):**
   - Juri atau penguji skripsi dapat mengenali angka teks, angka tabel asli, dan kesimpulan kesalahannya dalam $\le 3$ detik tanpa perlu membaca formula SciPy mentah.
2. **Presisi Penyorotan BBox:**
   - Tidak ada kotak highlight yang menabrak kalimat di atas atau di bawahnya. Kalimat 2 baris disorot per baris secara akurat.
3. **Keterbacaan Lintas Halaman:**
   - Setiap temuan lintas halaman (misal: klaim di Hal. 3, tabel di Hal. 2) menampilkan cuplikan mini baris tabel langsung di kartu kanan, mengeliminasi kebutuhan bolak-balik halaman secara manual.
4. **Bebas Tipografi AI-Slop:**
   - Tidak ada karakter strip panjang em dash, tidak ada kartu gradien ungu menyilaukan, dan seluruh teks antarmuka menggunakan bahasa Indonesia baku yang formal dan lugas.

# [SELESAI FILE: docs/PRD_UI_GOOGLE_STITCH.md]
################################################################################

################################################################################
# FILE [27/58]: frontend/app.js
# UKURAN: 69.3 KB | JUMLAH BARIS: 1710 | PERAN: Landing page controller, preset loader, simulation logic, and offline status indicator.
################################################################################
/**
 * LOCUS: Cursor for Scientific Integrity
 * Notion Style System: Warm Paper Notebook Under Afternoon Sun
 * Frontend Controller: Multi-Page Navigation, Precision X-Ray Bounding Boxes & Interactive Proofs
 * 100% Offline, Zero-PII, Zero-AGPL, Anti-Slop Clean
 */

let currentBundle = null;
let currentPresetIndex = 0;
let activeFindingId = null;
let presetsList = [];
let currentView = "landing";
let currentDocPage = 1;
let totalDocPages = 1;
let currentCanvasZoom = 1.0;
let currentFindingsFilter = "all";

// Authoritative Indonesian metadata for scientific verdict types
const VERDICT_META = {
    "POLARITY_INVERSION_ERROR": {
        title: "Inversi Polaritas Metrik",
        code: "POLARITAS",
        category: "error",
        desc: "Metrik galat (seperti RMSE/MAE) diinterpretasikan terbalik (makin kecil makin baik)."
    },
    "PREREQUISITE_LOGIC_INVERSION": {
        title: "Inversi Logika Uji Prasyarat",
        code: "LOGIKA-PRASYARAT",
        category: "error",
        desc: "Uji normalitas menyimpulkan normal padahal p < 0,05 (tolak H0, data tidak normal)."
    },
    "DIRECT_CELL_CONTRADICTION": {
        title: "Inkonsistensi Nilai Sel Tabel",
        code: "SEL-TABEL",
        category: "error",
        desc: "Nilai yang diklaim di narasi bertentangan langsung dengan angka di tabel bukti."
    },

function getVerdictMeta(verdict) {
    return VERDICT_META[verdict] || {
        title: (verdict || "TEMUAN").replace(/_/g, " "),
        code: "TEMUAN",
        category: ((verdict || "").includes("WARN") || (verdict || "").includes("NOTICE")) ? "warn" : (verdict === "PASS" ? "pass" : "error"),
        desc: "Temuan audit integritas numerik dokumen ilmiah."
    };
}

document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
    initPresets();
    setupDropzone();
    setupEventListeners();

    // Attach scroll listeners to update cross-pane Bézier connector
    const viewport = document.getElementById("pdf-viewport-container");
    const rightPane = document.querySelector(".pane-right");
    if (viewport) viewport.addEventListener("scroll", drawSvgConnector);
    if (rightPane) rightPane.addEventListener("scroll", drawSvgConnector);

    window.addEventListener("resize", () => {
        if (currentView === "studio") {
            handleResize();
            drawSvgConnector();
        }
    });
});

// View Navigation: 'landing' or 'studio'
function navigateTo(viewName) {
    if (viewName === "studio") {
        window.location.href = "/app/studio.html";
        return;
    }
    currentView = viewName;
    const landingView = document.getElementById("view-landing");
    const studioView = document.getElementById("view-studio");
    const navStudioBtn = document.getElementById("top-open-studio-btn");

    if (viewName === "landing") {
        if (landingView) landingView.style.display = "block";
        if (studioView) studioView.style.display = "none";
        if (navStudioBtn) navStudioBtn.style.display = "inline-flex";
        clearSvg();
        window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: "smooth" });
    } else {
        if (landingView) landingView.style.display = "none";
        if (studioView) studioView.style.display = "flex";
        if (navStudioBtn) navStudioBtn.style.display = "none";

        setTimeout(() => {
            handleResize();
            drawSvgConnector();
        }, 60);
    }
}

function scrollToSection(sectionId) {
    if (currentView !== "landing") {
        navigateTo("landing");
        setTimeout(() => {
            const el = document.getElementById(sectionId);
            if (el) el.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth" });
        }, 100);
    } else {
        const el = document.getElementById(sectionId);
        if (el) el.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth" });
    }
}

function openPresetInStudio(index) {
    window.location.href = `/app/studio.html?preset=${index}`;
}

function triggerPdfUpload() {
    const input = document.getElementById("pdf-file-input");
    if (input) input.click();
}

function handleResize() {
    const img = document.getElementById("pdf-page-img");
    const overlay = document.getElementById("xray-bounding-box-overlay");
    const svgEvidence = document.getElementById("page-evidence-svg");
    if (img && overlay && svgEvidence && currentBundle) {
        populateBoundingBoxes(overlay, svgEvidence, currentBundle, img);
    }
}

// Load 3 Golden Presets
async function initPresets() {
    try {
        const res = await fetch("/api/v1/presets");
        const data = await res.json();
        if (data.status === "success" && data.presets.length > 0) {
            presetsList = data.presets;
            renderPresetButtons();
            loadPreset(0, false);
        }
    } catch (err) {
        console.error("Gagal memuat presets:", err);
    }
}

function renderPresetButtons() {
    const container = document.getElementById("preset-buttons-container");
    if (!container) return;
    container.innerHTML = "";
    presetsList.forEach((p, idx) => {
        const btn = document.createElement("button");
        btn.className = `btn-preset ${idx === currentPresetIndex ? "active" : ""}`;
        btn.id = `preset-btn-${idx}`;
        btn.innerHTML = `<span style="font-weight:700">${p.id}</span>: ${p.title}`;
        btn.onclick = () => loadPreset(idx, true);
        container.appendChild(btn);
    });
}

let loadingTimer = null;
let loadingStartTime = 0;
let activeAuditAbortController = null;
let activeAuditPollInterval = null;
