# FILE [55/58]: tests/test_stat_solvers.py
# UKURAN: 6.9 KB | JUMLAH BARIS: 194 | PERAN: Tests for Statcheck (t/F/r/chi2/Z), GRIM, GRIMMER, Z-formula, and Debit/Credit solvers.
################################################################################
"""
Unit Tests for LOCUS Deterministic Mathematical Solvers.
Ensures 100% accuracy on statistical formulas and consistency bounds.
"""

import pytest
from backend.layer2_core.stat_solvers import (
    StatcheckSolver,
    GuardedGrimSolver,
    GrimmerSolver,
    DebitSolver,
    TotalNSubgroupSweeper,
    ConfusionMatrixAndSumAuditor
)


def test_statcheck_t_pass():
    # t(28) = 2.45, reported p = 0.021
    res = StatcheckSolver.solve(
        test_type="t",
        stat_val=2.45,
        df1=28,
        p_reported=0.021,
        stat_str="2.45"
    )
    assert res["verdict"] == "PASS"
    assert res["p_interval"][0] <= 0.021 <= res["p_interval"][1]


def test_statcheck_decision_flip():
    # t(30) = 1.60 -> two-tailed p ≈ 0.120 (not significant, p >= 0.05)
    # but reported as 0.02 (significant, p < 0.05) -> DECISION FLIP
    res = StatcheckSolver.solve(
        test_type="t",
        stat_val=1.60,
        df1=30,
        p_reported=0.02,
        stat_str="1.60"
    )
    assert res["verdict"] == "DECISION_FLIP"


def test_statcheck_one_tailed_context():
    # Context contains 'satu arah'
    res = StatcheckSolver.solve(
        test_type="t",
        stat_val=2.00,
        df1=30,
        p_reported=0.027,
        stat_str="2.00",
        context_text="Pengujian dilakukan secara satu arah (one-tailed) dengan hipotesis terarah."
    )
    assert res["is_one_tailed"] is True
    assert res["verdict"] == "PASS"


def test_statcheck_apa_precision():
    res = StatcheckSolver.solve(
        test_type="F",
        stat_val=15.2,
        df1=2,
        df2=45,
        p_reported=0.000,
        stat_str="15.2"
    )
    assert res["verdict"] == "APA_PRECISION_NOTICE"


def test_guarded_grim_pass_and_fail():
    # With N=20 and 1 item: means must be multiples of 1/20 = 0.05
    # Mean = 3.45 is valid (3.45 * 20 = 69)
    res_pass = GuardedGrimSolver.solve(mean_val=3.45, n_sample=20, k_items=1, mean_str="3.45")
    assert res_pass["verdict"] == "PASS"

    # Mean = 3.47 is impossible on N=20 (nearest sum is 69 -> 3.45 or 70 -> 3.50)
    res_fail = GuardedGrimSolver.solve(mean_val=3.47, n_sample=20, k_items=1, mean_str="3.47")
    assert res_fail["verdict"] == "GRIM_INCONSISTENCY"


def test_guarded_grim_applicability():
    # Continuous variables shouldn't trigger GRIM
    res = GuardedGrimSolver.solve(mean_val=1250000.50, n_sample=30, is_discrete=False)
    assert res["verdict"] == "GRIM_NOT_APPLICABLE"


def test_grimmer_parity():
    # Valid N=20, mean=3.00 (S=60 is even).
    # With SD=1.03, (20-1)*1.03^2 + 60^2/20 = 19*1.0609 + 180 = 20.157 + 180 = 200.157
    # Nearest integer Q = 200 (even). S=60 (even). Q ≡ S (mod 2) -> PASSES GRIMMER!
    res_pass = GrimmerSolver.solve(mean_val=3.00, sd_val=1.03, n_sample=20, sd_str="1.03")
    assert res_pass["verdict"] == "PASS"

    # With SD=1.00, SS = 19*1 + 180 = 199 (odd). But S=60 (even). Parity mismatch -> GRIMMER_INCONSISTENCY!
    res_fail = GrimmerSolver.solve(mean_val=3.00, sd_val=1.00, n_sample=20, sd_str="1.00")
    assert res_fail["verdict"] == "GRIMMER_INCONSISTENCY"


def test_debit_binary():
    # In N=50, proportion 0.40 (k=20), binary SD = sqrt(50/49 * 0.4 * 0.6) = sqrt(0.24489) = 0.4948 ~ 0.49
    res_pass = DebitSolver.solve(prop_val=0.40, sd_val=0.49, n_sample=50, prop_str="0.40", sd_str="0.49")
    assert res_pass["verdict"] == "PASS"

    # Inconsistent SD
    res_fail = DebitSolver.solve(prop_val=0.40, sd_val=0.15, n_sample=50, prop_str="0.40", sd_str="0.15")
    assert res_fail["verdict"] == "DEBIT_INCONSISTENCY"


def test_total_n_subgroup_sweeper():
    # Total N = 50, mean1 = 3.45, mean2 = 3.50
    res = TotalNSubgroupSweeper.sweep(mean1=3.45, mean2=3.50, total_n=50)
    assert res["verdict"] == "PASS"
    assert res["feasible_count"] > 0


def test_confusion_matrix_auditor():
    # TP=80, FP=20, FN=10, TN=90
    # Precision = 80 / 100 = 0.80
    # Recall = 80 / 90 = 0.8889
    # Accuracy = 170 / 200 = 0.85
    res = ConfusionMatrixAndSumAuditor.audit_confusion_matrix(
        tp=80, fp=20, tn=90, fn=10,
        reported_precision=0.80,
        reported_recall=0.89,
        reported_accuracy=0.85
    )
    assert res["verdict"] == "PASS"


def test_percentage_sum():
    res_pass = ConfusionMatrixAndSumAuditor.audit_percentage_sum([25.4, 34.6, 40.0])
    assert res_pass["verdict"] == "PASS"

    res_fail = ConfusionMatrixAndSumAuditor.audit_percentage_sum([20.0, 30.0, 45.0]) # sum 95%
    assert res_fail["verdict"] == "SUM_PERCENTAGE_ERROR"


def test_tuple_verification_solver():
    from backend.layer2_core.stat_solvers import TupleVerificationSolver

    # 1. Exact match pass
    p1 = TupleVerificationSolver.verify_claimed_cell_number("1,49", 1.4874307, "Mean", "SQRTUKAP")
    assert p1["verdict"] == "PASS"

    # 2. Truncation rounding warning (2.07593 truncated to 2.07 instead of 2.08)
    w1 = TupleVerificationSolver.verify_claimed_cell_number("2,67", 2.6759333, "Mean", "SQRTPGLMN")
    assert w1["verdict"] == "WARN_TRUNCATION_ROUNDING"

    # 3. Transposition / Typo (5.48 typed as 5.84)
    t1 = TupleVerificationSolver.verify_claimed_cell_number("5,84", 5.48128, "Maximum", "PNGLM")
    assert t1["verdict"] == "DIGIT_TRANSPOSITION_TYPO"

    # 4. Direct cell contradiction (1.38 reported as 1.49)
    c1 = TupleVerificationSolver.verify_claimed_cell_number("1,49", 1.38495, "Mean", "SQRTKAP")
    assert c1["verdict"] == "DIRECT_CELL_CONTRADICTION"


def test_formula_eval_solver():
    from backend.layer2_core.stat_solvers import FormulaEvalSolver

    # 1. Zskewness pass: 0.317 / sqrt(6/68) = 1.067
    p1 = FormulaEvalSolver.audit_z_formula("Skewness", 0.317, 68, 1.067, 1.067)
    assert p1["verdict"] == "PASS"

    # 2. Zkurtosis narrative contradiction: formula output 0.71, text claims 1.05
    c1 = FormulaEvalSolver.audit_z_formula("Kurtosis", 0.420, 68, 0.71, 1.05)
    assert c1["verdict"] == "INLINE_FORMULA_NARRATIVE_CONTRADICTION"

    # 3. Zskewness calculation error: 0.267 / sqrt(6/68) reported as 1.063 (exact is 0.899)
    e1 = FormulaEvalSolver.audit_z_formula("Skewness", 0.267, 68, 1.063, 1.063)
    assert e1["verdict"] == "INLINE_FORMULA_CALC_ERROR"


def test_formula_sympy_ast_normalizer():
    from backend.layer2_core.stat_solvers import FormulaEvalSolver

    # Test Unicode square root and Indonesian comma decimal
    res = FormulaEvalSolver.evaluate_symbolic_expression("0,420 / √(24/68)")
    assert res["status"] == "SUCCESS"
    assert round(res["result"], 2) == 0.71

    # Test context variable substitution
    res_var = FormulaEvalSolver.evaluate_symbolic_expression("x / sqrt(6/N)", {"x": 0.317, "N": 68})
    assert res_var["status"] == "SUCCESS"
    assert round(res_var["result"], 3) == 1.067


def test_ocr_numeric_post_corrector():
    from backend.layer1_perception.ocr_fallback import OcrTableFallback

    corrected = OcrTableFallback.correct_numeric_ocr("O.425")
    assert corrected == "0.425"

    corrected_l = OcrTableFallback.correct_numeric_ocr("1.3l8")
    assert corrected_l == "1.318"

"""
Unit Tests for LOCUS Table Extraction and Number Normalization.
Ensures accurate cell parsing, 3-line table extraction, and dual-locale detection.
"""

import os
import pytest
from backend.layer1_perception.number_normalizer import NumberNormalizer
from backend.layer1_perception.pdf_reader import FastPdfReader


def test_dual_locale_column_detection():
    # Indonesian PUEBI column
    id_col = ["12,45", "0,018", "1.250,00", "45,80"]
    assert NumberNormalizer.detect_column_locale(id_col) == "ID_PUEBI"

    # SPSS US column
    spss_col = [".000", ".018", "12.45", "1,250.00"]
    assert NumberNormalizer.detect_column_locale(spss_col) == "SPSS_US"


def test_parse_numeric_tokens():
    # SPSS leading dot
    p1 = NumberNormalizer.parse_numeric(".000", "SPSS_US")
    assert p1 is not None
    assert p1["float_val"] == 0.0
    assert p1["decimals"] == 3

    # Indonesian comma
    p2 = NumberNormalizer.parse_numeric("12,45", "ID_PUEBI")
    assert p2 is not None
    assert p2["float_val"] == 12.45
    assert p2["decimals"] == 2

    # Number with significance asterisk
    p3 = NumberNormalizer.parse_numeric("0,018**", "ID_PUEBI")
    assert p3 is not None
    assert p3["float_val"] == 0.018


def test_preset_pdf_extraction():
    preset_1_path = os.path.join(
        os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))),
        "data", "presets", "preset_1_ml_polarity.pdf"
    )
    assert os.path.exists(preset_1_path)

    doc_graph = FastPdfReader.process_pdf(preset_1_path)
    assert doc_graph["num_pages"] >= 1
    assert len(doc_graph["tables"]) >= 1
    table = doc_graph["tables"][0]
    assert table["num_cols"] == 4
    assert len(table["rows"]) == 3

# FILE [57/58]: tests/test_v351_hardening.py
# UKURAN: 3.9 KB | JUMLAH BARIS: 91 | PERAN: Tests for polarity guard negation fix, contextual NIM redactor, and Statcheck ANOVA.

"""
Regression unit tests for LOCUS v3.5.1 Hardening.
Verifies negation polarity, citation year extraction, Statcheck ANOVA decision flip,
PII redactor decimal preservation, and guarded GRIM sample size abstention.
"""

import pytest


def test_polarity_guard_negation_normality():
    """1. Legitimate non-normal statements (p < 0.05) must not trigger PREREQUISITE_LOGIC_INVERSION."""
    from backend.layer2_core.polarity_guard import PolarityGuard

    guard = PolarityGuard()
    text = "Data tidak berdistribusi normal karena nilai Sig. Kolmogorov-Smirnov sebesar 0,012 < 0,05."
    res = guard.check_prerequisite_inversion(text)
    assert res is None, f"Expected None for legitimate negation statement, got {res}"


def test_semantic_numeric_guard_year_prefix():
    """2. 'Pada 2021' must be classified as CITATION_YEAR and prevented from statistical linking."""
    from src.core.semantic_numeric_guard import classify_numeric_span

    sentence = "Pada 2021, nilai rata-rata DELAY sebesar 76,40."
    start_2021 = sentence.find("2021")
    end_2021 = start_2021 + len("2021")
    cls_2021 = classify_numeric_span(sentence, start_2021, end_2021, "2021")
    assert cls_2021 == "CITATION_YEAR", f"Expected CITATION_YEAR for 'Pada 2021', got {cls_2021}"

    start_val = sentence.find("76,40")
    end_val = start_val + len("76,40")
    cls_val = classify_numeric_span(sentence, start_val, end_val, "76,40")
    assert cls_val == "STAT_METRIC", f"Expected STAT_METRIC for '76,40', got {cls_val}"


def test_statcheck_anova_decision_flip():
    """3. ANOVA F(1, 50) = 2.10 with reported p = 0.005 must trigger DECISION_FLIP in CellLinker."""
    from backend.layer2_core.cell_linker import CellLinker

    linker = CellLinker()
    claim = {
        "claim_id": "C_TEST_ANOVA",
        "page": 1,
        "text": "Uji ANOVA menghasilkan F(1, 50) = 2,10 dengan p = 0,005.",
        "bbox": [50, 100, 300, 120]
    }
    findings = linker.link_claim_to_table(claim, [])
    assert len(findings) > 0, "Expected a finding for ANOVA statcheck error"
    flip_finding = next((f for f in findings if f.get("verdict") == "DECISION_FLIP"), None)
    assert flip_finding is not None, f"Expected DECISION_FLIP finding, got {[f.get('verdict') for f in findings]}"
    assert flip_finding["severity"] == "CRITICAL"


def test_pii_redactor_spss_decimal_and_nim():
    """4. PII redactor must keep long SPSS floats intact while redacting labeled NIM."""
    from backend.layer3_export.pii_redactor import PiiRedactor

    raw = "Mean = 1.487430741340253, NIM: 1512622045"
    redacted = PiiRedactor.redact_text(raw)
    assert "1.487430741340253" in redacted, f"SPSS floating decimal was corrupted: {redacted}"
    assert "1512622045" not in redacted, f"NIM was not redacted: {redacted}"
    assert "NIM: 1512[NIM-REDACTED]" in redacted


def test_cell_linker_mean_without_n_abstains():
    """5. CellLinker on mean claim without explicit N or table N column must abstain from GRIM."""
    from backend.layer2_core.cell_linker import CellLinker

    linker = CellLinker()
    claim = {
        "claim_id": "C_TEST_GRIM",
        "page": 1,
        "text": "Nilai rata-rata persepsi adalah 3,43.",
        "bbox": [50, 100, 300, 120]
    }
    table = {
        "table_id": "Tabel_1",
        "page": 1,
        "num_rows": 1,
        "num_cols": 2,
        "header_paths": ["Variabel", "Mean"],
        "rows": [
            [
                {"row_idx": 0, "col_idx": 0, "raw_val": "Persepsi", "num_val": None, "header_path": "Variabel", "row_label": "Persepsi"},
                {"row_idx": 0, "col_idx": 1, "raw_val": "3.43", "num_val": 3.43, "header_path": "Mean", "row_label": "Persepsi"}
            ]
        ]
    }
    findings = linker.link_claim_to_table(claim, [table], detected_total_n=None)
    grim_findings = [f for f in findings if f.get("verdict") == "GRIM_INCONSISTENCY"]
    assert len(grim_findings) == 0, f"Expected 0 GRIM false positives when N is absent, got {grim_findings}"

# FILE [58/58]: tests/test_v35_semantic_audit.py
# UKURAN: 10.1 KB | JUMLAH BARIS: 258 | PERAN: Tests for citation year suppression, textbook rule suppression, and descriptive sentences.
################################################################################
"""
Regression Test Suite for LOCUS v3.5 Semantic Type-Safe & Cross-Encoder Grounding Engine.
Verifies:
1. Anti-year and citation suppression (e.g. data tahun 2019 sampai 2021 menurut Ghozali 2021 skala 1 sampai 5).
2. Textbook rule suppression (e.g. VIF < 10 dan tolerance > 0.10).
3. Bab IV descriptive inequality and aposition (> 30 yaitu 76.4).
4. Contrastive clause split multi-variable binding (DELAY vs SQRTUKAP).
5. Layer 1 perception and Layer 2 core hardening (row labels, Likert sum, FastPdfReader callback).
"""

import os
import pytest
from src.core.semantic_numeric_guard import (
    is_non_data_sentence,
    classify_numeric_span,
)
from src.core.quad_tuple_verifier import (
    extract_quad_tuples_from_sentence,
    verify_quad_tuple_against_table,
    ClaimQuadTuple,
)
from backend.layer2_core.cell_linker import (
    is_invalid_row_label,
    match_sentence_to_row,
)
from src.core.spss_likert_engine import (
    check_likert_row_sum_in_sentence,
    CleanedSPSSRow,
)
from backend.layer1_perception.pdf_reader import FastPdfReader


def test_anti_year_and_citation_suppression():
    """
    Test 1: Verification of methodology sentence with citation year and scale:
    'Penelitian ini menggunakan data tahun 2019 sampai 2021 menurut Ghozali (2021) dengan skala 1 sampai 5.'
    against table with variable DELAY.
    Ensures:
    - is_non_data_sentence returns True.
    - No claim quad tuple with value 2019, 2021, 1, or 5 is permitted.
    - No error finding is produced.
    """
    sentence = "Penelitian ini menggunakan data tahun 2019 sampai 2021 menurut Ghozali (2021) dengan skala 1 sampai 5."
    assert is_non_data_sentence(sentence) is True

    quads = extract_quad_tuples_from_sentence(
        sentence,
        candidate_table_variables=["DELAY", "Ukuran Perusahaan"],
        default_variable="DELAY"
    )
    # Ensure no quad with value 2019, 2021, 1, or 5 is extracted
    suppressed_vals = {2019.0, 2021.0, 1.0, 5.0}
    extracted_suppressed = [q for q in quads if q.numeric_value in suppressed_vals]
    assert len(extracted_suppressed) == 0

    table_headers = ["Variabel", "N", "Minimum", "Maximum", "Mean", "Std. Deviation"]
    table_rows = [
        ["DELAY", "68", "56.00", "97.00", "76.40", "9.395"]
    ]
    # Even if quads were somehow extracted, verify no finding error
    for q in quads:
        res = verify_quad_tuple_against_table(q, table_headers, table_rows)
        assert res.status != "NUMERICAL_CONTRADICTION"


def test_textbook_rule_suppression():
    """
    Test 2: Verification of textbook rule / decision criterion sentence:
    'Kriteria uji multikolinearitas adalah nilai VIF < 10 dan tolerance > 0,10 dengan taraf signifikansi 5%.'
    Ensures:
    - is_non_data_sentence returns True.
    - Tokens are classified as SCALE_OR_RULE_THRESHOLD.
    - No claim quad tuple is generated, and zero error findings produced.
    """
    sentence = "Kriteria uji multikolinearitas adalah nilai VIF < 10 dan tolerance > 0,10 dengan taraf signifikansi 5%."
    assert is_non_data_sentence(sentence) is True

    quads = extract_quad_tuples_from_sentence(
        sentence,
        candidate_table_variables=["VIF", "Tolerance"],
        default_variable=None
    )
    assert len(quads) == 0


def test_bab4_descriptive_inequality_and_aposition():
    """
    Test 3: Real Bab IV sentence with inequality (> 30) and glued token (76,4dengan)
    'Rata-rata sampel yang diteliti menunjukkan terjadi keterlambatan prosedur audit yang cukup besar yang terlihat dari nilai rata-rata (mean) > 30 yaitu 76,4dengan standar deviasi sebesar 9,395.'
    Matched against DELAY row (Mean = 76.40, Std. Deviation = 9.395).
    Ensures:
    - '> 30' verified as VALID_EXACT (76.40 > 30 is True).
    - '76.4' verified as VALID_EXACT (equal to 76.40).
    - '9.395' verified as VALID_EXACT.
    - Zero error findings.
    """
    sentence = (
        "Rata-rata sampel yang diteliti menunjukkan terjadi keterlambatan prosedur audit yang cukup besar "
        "yang terlihat dari nilai rata-rata (mean) > 30 yaitu 76,4dengan standar deviasi sebesar 9,395."
    )
    quads = extract_quad_tuples_from_sentence(
        sentence,
        candidate_table_variables=["DELAY", "Audit Delay"],
        default_variable="DELAY"
    )
    assert len(quads) == 3

    quad_30 = next((q for q in quads if abs(q.numeric_value - 30.0) < 0.01), None)
    assert quad_30 is not None
    assert quad_30.operator == "GT"
    assert quad_30.stat_type == "MEAN"

    quad_mean = next((q for q in quads if abs(q.numeric_value - 76.4) < 0.01), None)
    assert quad_mean is not None
    assert quad_mean.operator == "EQ"
    assert quad_mean.stat_type == "MEAN"

    quad_sd = next((q for q in quads if abs(q.numeric_value - 9.395) < 0.001), None)
    assert quad_sd is not None
    assert quad_sd.operator == "EQ"
    assert quad_sd.stat_type == "STD_DEV"

    table_headers = ["Variabel", "N", "Minimum", "Maximum", "Mean", "Std. Deviation"]
    table_rows = [
        ["DELAY", "68", "56.00", "97.00", "76.40", "9.395"]
    ]

    res_30 = verify_quad_tuple_against_table(quad_30, table_headers, table_rows)
    assert res_30.status == "VALID_EXACT"

    res_mean = verify_quad_tuple_against_table(quad_mean, table_headers, table_rows)
    assert res_mean.status == "VALID_EXACT"

    res_sd = verify_quad_tuple_against_table(quad_sd, table_headers, table_rows)
    assert res_sd.status == "VALID_EXACT"


def test_contrastive_clause_split_multi_variable():
    """
    Test 4: Real Bab IV multi-variable contrastive sentence:
    'Nilai DELAY berkisar antara 56 sampai 97, sedangkan SQRTUKAP memiliki nilai tertinggi 1,73 dan terendah 1,00 dengan rata-rata 1,49.'
    Matched against DELAY (Min=56, Max=97) and SQRTUKAP (Min=1.00, Max=1.73, Mean=1.49).
    Ensures:
    - DELAY binds 56 as MINIMUM and 97 as MAXIMUM.
    - SQRTUKAP binds 1.73 as MAXIMUM, 1.00 as MINIMUM, and 1.49 as MEAN.
    - No variable or column swapping.
    """
    sentence = (
        "Nilai DELAY berkisar antara 56 sampai 97, sedangkan SQRTUKAP memiliki nilai "
        "tertinggi 1,73 dan terendah 1,00 dengan rata-rata 1,49."
    )
    quads = extract_quad_tuples_from_sentence(
        sentence,
        candidate_table_variables=["DELAY", "SQRTUKAP"]
    )
    assert len(quads) == 5

    delay_quads = [q for q in quads if q.variable_name == "DELAY"]
    sqrt_quads = [q for q in quads if q.variable_name == "SQRTUKAP"]

    assert len(delay_quads) == 2
    assert len(sqrt_quads) == 3

    delay_min = next((q for q in delay_quads if q.stat_type == "MINIMUM"), None)
    assert delay_min is not None
    assert abs(delay_min.numeric_value - 56.0) < 0.01

    delay_max = next((q for q in delay_quads if q.stat_type == "MAXIMUM"), None)
    assert delay_max is not None
    assert abs(delay_max.numeric_value - 97.0) < 0.01

    sqrt_max = next((q for q in sqrt_quads if q.stat_type == "MAXIMUM"), None)
    assert sqrt_max is not None
    assert abs(sqrt_max.numeric_value - 1.73) < 0.01

    sqrt_min = next((q for q in sqrt_quads if q.stat_type == "MINIMUM"), None)
    assert sqrt_min is not None
    assert abs(sqrt_min.numeric_value - 1.00) < 0.01

    sqrt_mean = next((q for q in sqrt_quads if q.stat_type == "MEAN"), None)
    assert sqrt_mean is not None
    assert abs(sqrt_mean.numeric_value - 1.49) < 0.01

    table_headers = ["Variabel", "N", "Minimum", "Maximum", "Mean", "Std. Deviation"]
    table_rows = [
        ["DELAY", "68", "56.00", "97.00", "76.40", "9.395"],
        ["SQRTUKAP", "68", "1.00", "1.73", "1.49", "0.18"]
    ]

    for q in quads:
        res = verify_quad_tuple_against_table(q, table_headers, table_rows)
        assert res.status == "VALID_EXACT"
        assert res.matched_row_label == q.variable_name


def test_layer1_and_layer2_hardening():
    """
    Test 5: Layer 1 perception and Layer 2 core hardening checks:
    - X1, X2 not discarded by is_invalid_row_label().
    - VALID and MISSING discarded by is_invalid_row_label().
    - ROW_SUM_MISMATCH detected when total baris != jumlah klausa.
    - FastPdfReader.process_pdf receives callback with 4 arguments (current, total, step, pct) without TypeError.
    """
    assert is_invalid_row_label("X1") is False
    assert is_invalid_row_label("X2") is False
    assert is_invalid_row_label("CR") is False
    assert is_invalid_row_label("PB") is False

    assert is_invalid_row_label("VALID") is True
    assert is_invalid_row_label("MISSING") is True
    assert is_invalid_row_label("TOTAL") is True
    assert is_invalid_row_label("1") is True

    # ROW_SUM_MISMATCH check
    sentence = "Sebanyak 12 responden menjawab sangat setuju dan 18 responden menjawab setuju."
    table_cleaned = {
        "SS": CleanedSPSSRow(
            row_code="SS",
            raw_label="Sangat Setuju",
            cells={"FREQUENCY": 12.0, "PERCENT": 30.0},
            raw_cells={"FREQUENCY": "12", "PERCENT": "30,0"}
        ),
        "S": CleanedSPSSRow(
            row_code="S",
            raw_label="Setuju",
            cells={"FREQUENCY": 18.0, "PERCENT": 45.0},
            raw_cells={"FREQUENCY": "18", "PERCENT": "45,0"}
        ),
        "TOTAL": CleanedSPSSRow(
            row_code="TOTAL",
            raw_label="Total",
            cells={"FREQUENCY": 40.0, "PERCENT": 100.0},
            raw_cells={"FREQUENCY": "40", "PERCENT": "100,0"}
        )
    }
    sum_check = check_likert_row_sum_in_sentence(sentence, table_cleaned)
    assert sum_check is not None
    assert sum_check["verdict"] == "ROW_SUM_MISMATCH"
    assert sum_check["claimed_sum"] == 30.0
    assert sum_check["table_total"] == 40.0

    # FastPdfReader callback test
    preset_pdf = os.path.join("data", "presets", "preset_1_ml_polarity.pdf")
    assert os.path.exists(preset_pdf), f"Preset PDF not found at {preset_pdf}"

    callback_calls = []
    def mock_hook(current: int, total: int, step: str, pct: int):
        callback_calls.append((current, total, step, pct))

    doc_graph = FastPdfReader.process_pdf(preset_pdf, progress_callback=mock_hook)
    assert "claims" in doc_graph
    assert "tables" in doc_graph
    assert len(callback_calls) > 0
    cur, tot, step, pct = callback_calls[0]
    assert isinstance(cur, int)
    assert isinstance(tot, int)
    assert isinstance(step, str)
    assert isinstance(pct, int)
